#933. Persaingan Gelar Premier League Semakin Ketat Arsenal Mengalahkan City di Pertemuan Tengah Pekan

Arsenal kembali ke puncak klasemen dengan kemenangan 2-1 atas Manchester City pada hari ke-26, sementara Wolves menghentikan streak 21 pertandingan tanpa kemenangan Burnley di Turf Moor. Hasil ini memutarbalik pertempuran Top Skor dan menegangkan persaingan gelar dengan tujuh pertandingan tersisa.

Emirates Stadium: Arsenal Mengambil Kembali Puncak

Para pengemis utara London menghasilkan performa klinis di bawah penerangan gelap untuk membalik defisit separuh pertama dan mengalahkan Manchester City 2-1 di Emirates Stadium pada malam Rabu. Gabriel Martinelli dari Arsenal membuka skor dalam menit ke-23, hanya untuk Erling Haaland dari City menyamakan dari titik penalty sebelum bertahan di pertengahan babak. Momen putus-putus terjadi pada menit ke-68 ketika pengganti Kai Havertz mengalihkan kepala ke gol dari silang Bukayo Saka, memicu penghormatan gembira di kalangan pengemis rumah. Kemenangan ini memindahkan Arsenal sejajar dengan City pada 63 poin tetapi di atasnya perolehan gol, memulihkan persaingan gelar dengan tujuh pekan lagi. Pelatih Mikel Arteta memuji ketahanan pihaknya, menyatakan kemenangan ini "menunjukkan karakter kumpulan ini" dalam musim yang ditentukan oleh jarak yang sempit.

Kerusakan Etihad: Deduction Poin Mengancam saat Acara Terus

Di luar lapangan, Manchester City menghadapi krisis yang semakin memburuk saat komision mandiri Premier League terus melanjutkan proses penetapan terhadap 115 tuduhan fair play finansial yang dikenakan kepada klub. Sidang terbaru, yang diselenggarakan pada Selasa, menggambarkan deduction poin potensial berkisar antara enam hingga sepuluh poin jika komisi menemukan dakwaan terbukti. Pelatih dan eksekutif pesaing telah mengungkapkan frustrasi, dengan Eddie Howe dari Newcastle United mendeskripsikan situasi sebagai "distraksi yang merusak integritas kompetisi." Ketidakpastian ini sudah mulai memengaruhi perencanaan City, dengan pelatih Enzo Maresca mengkonfirmasi bahwa lima pemain tim pertama sedang ditandai untuk istirahat di depan jadwal yang menuntut, gerakan yang dipaksa oleh kebutuhan tim untuk menjaga kebugaran di tengah gangguan di luar lapangan. Kepercayaan para penggemar masih rapuh, dan ambisi Eropa klub tergantung se selembar laporan akhir komisi.

Turf Moor: Wolves Memutus Streak Tidak Kemenangan

Di Turf Moor, Wolves memperoleh kemenangan vital 2-1 atas Burnley, menghentikan streak rumah tanpa kemenangan di liga yang telah bertahan 21 pertandingan. Fer Lopez membuka skor untuk Wolves dalam menit ke-34, mengalihkan kepala ke gol dari corner sebelum Josh Cullen dari Burnley menyamakan tepat sebelum habis babak. Gol menang datang di akhir babak kedua, dengan captain Ruben Neves dari Wolves mengubah free-kick presisi dari 25 yard ke dalam gol pada menit ke-87. Hasil ini memindahkan Wolves keluar dari zona relegasi dan ke tempat ke-14, sementara Burnley tetap menempati dasar klasemen dengan 18 poin dari 26 pertemuan. Pelatih Vincent Kompany mengakui pihaknya "kembang percaya" selama babak pertama tetapi memuji karakter tim untuk perlawanan di babak kedua. Menang ini memberikan ruang napas yang krusial bagi Wolves dalam pertempuran penyelamatan, dengan klub sekarang empat poin di atas zona turun kelas.

Goodison Park: Chelsea Mengalahkan Everton di Derby London

Chelsea memperoleh kemenangan 2-1 di Goodison Park, memindahkan diri keengah klasemen dan menjaga aspiration Eropa mereka hidup. Nicolas Jackson memimpin Chelsea ke dalam dalam menit ke-12, menyelesaikan kontra serangan cepat yang menangkap pertahanan Everton yang tinggi di padang. Everton merespons sebelum bertahan melalui Dominic Calvert-Lewin, yang mengangkat paling tinggi untuk menemui cross dari Anthony Gordon. Momen putus-putus tiba segera setelah bertahan ketika Mateo Kovačić dari Chelsea mengintersep di tengah dan mendorong ke depan untuk menyematkan gol pemenang pada menit ke-55. Hasil ini melihat Chelsea mendaki ke tempat ke-11 dengan 38 poin, sementara Everton berada di tempat ke-10 pada 39 poin, setara pada gol dibuat tetapi di belakang pada gol tamu. Pelatih Enzo Maresca menekankan pentingnya tiga poin, menegaskan "intensitas dan aplikasi" kumpulan menjadi faktor kunci dalam pertandingan di mana "keduanya memiliki kesempatan untuk mengambil kendali."

Pertandingan Top Skor: Haaland Memimpin tetapi Tekanan Membesar

Standing Top Skor menjadi semakin kompetitif setelah pertandingan tengah pekan. Erling Haaland tetap menjadi pemimpin mutlak dengan 24 gol dari 25 pertemuan, meskipun catatannya mencakup penalty melawan Arsenal. Bukayo Saka dari Arsenal ditempatkan kedua pada 18 gol, setelah menemukan jaring dua kali dalam kemenangan 2-1 atas City, sementara haul 15 gol Gabriel Martinelli telah ditambah dengan asistnya untuk gol pembukaan di Emirates Stadium. Alexander Isak dari Newcastle dan Mohamed Salah dari Liverpool masing-masing berada pada 16 gol, dengan kehadiran konsisten Isak di ketiga terakhir membuatnya kandidat gelap untuk penghargaan. Dengan tujuh ronde tersisa, persaingan siap untuk akhir yang menarik, dan penurunan form dari pemimpin utama bisa melihat penghargaan yang dicari berganti tangan sebelum musim berakhir.

Apa yang Harus Dilihat: Tujuh Pekan Akhir

Stretch tujuh pekan akhir musim Premier League janji drama di banyak bidang. Arsenal akan mencoba membangun momentum ketika menghadap Nottingham Forest di City Ground pada Sabtu, sekelompok yang saat ini berada di tempat Eropa tetapi rentan terhadap kontra serangan. Manchester City, menghadapi kemungkinan deduction poin, harus navigasi daftar pertemuan yang mencakup kunjungan ke Anfield dan Stamford Bridge, hasil yang bisa menentukan persaingan gelar. Di pertempuran penyelamatan, streak tanpa kemenangan Burnley dan peningkatan form Wolves menyiapkan finale yang ketegangan, dengan Clarets menghadap Newcastle United pada hari terakhir mengetahui kekalahan bisa mengkonfirmasi nasib mereka. Persaingan Top Skor juga bergantung pada benang halus, dengan kebugaran Haaland dan form Saka menjadi narasi utama. Saat liga memasuki akhir bisnis, setiap poin membawa bobot yang ditambah, dan narasi yang terungkit di atas dan di bawah lapangan akan membentuk babak akhir dari musim 2025-26.

#934. แรงกดดัน AI อิสระสั่นธุรกิจไทย 'ธรรมาภิบาล' กลายอุปสรรคใหญ่

รัฐบาลไทยเผชิญแรงกดดันจาก AI อิสระที่เพิ่มความเร็ว ทำให้องค์กรต้องปรับโครงสร้างการทำงานและจัดสรรงบประมาณไปกับการดิจิทัลทรัพย์สิน กลไก "ผลผลิตเพิ่มขึ้นแต่ค่าใช้จ่ายเพิ่มตาม" ส่งผลให้ค่าใช้จ่ายบำรุงรักษา AI เพิ่มขึ้น 25% ของงบประมาณเทคโนโลยี...

รัฐบาลไทยกำลังเผชิญแรงกดดันจากการพัฒนาระบบ AI อิสระที่เพิ่มความเร็วขึ้น ทำให้โมเดลภาษาและระบบอัตโนมัติสามารถเข้ามาแทนที่งานที่เคยทำโดยมนุษย์ได้ ซึ่งส่งผลให้องค์กรต้องปรับโครงสร้างการทำงานทั้งหมดในเร็วๆ นี้ กลไกนี้ทำให้บริษัทต้องเผชิญกับค่าใช้จ่ายในการปรับปรุงระบบพื้นฐานและการฝึกอบรมพนักงานใหม่ ทำให้งบประมาณที่จัดสรรไว้สำหรับการขยายธุรกิจต้องถูกจัดสรรใหม่ไปกับการดิจิทัลทรัพย์สิน

กลไกสำคัญที่ทำให้เกิดผลกระทบคือ "ผลผลิตเพิ่มขึ้นแต่ค่าใช้จ่ายเพิ่มตาม" ซึ่งในทางเศรษฐศาสตร์หมายความว่าแม้ AI จะสามารถเพิ่มผลผลิตได้ถึง 40% ตามที่องค์กร OECD รายงาน แต่ค่าใช้จ่ายในการบำรุงรักษาและอัปเดตโมเดล AI ก็อาจเพิ่มขึ้นถึง 25% ของงบประมาณเทคโนโลยีของบริษัท ทำให้มูลค่าผลตอบแทนสุทธิ (Net ROI) ลดลง หากบริษัทไม่สามารถควบคุม "กฎหมาย–ธรรมาภิบาล" ที่เกี่ยวข้องกับการใช้ข้อมูลส่วนบุคคลและความปลอดภัยของ AI ได้อย่างมีประสิทธิภาพ

ข้อมูลจากบันทึกของกรมพัฒนาวิทยาศาสตร์และเทคโนโลยีแสดงให้เห็นว่า บริษัทไทยที่นำ AI มาใช้แล้วรายงานว่าประสิทธิภาพการทำงานเพิ่มขึ้นเฉลี่ย 30% แต่ก็มีปัญหาเกี่ยวกับ "ความปลอดภัยของข้อมูล" ที่เพิ่มขึ้น 35% ในรอบ 12 เดือนที่ผ่านมา ซึ่งทำให้ต้องใช้จ่ายเพิ่มเติมสำหรับระบบรักษาความปลอดภัยดังกล่าว นอกจากนี้ รายงานจากบริษัทที่ปรึกษา McKinsey ยังชี้ให้เห็นว่า หากองค์กรไม่มีกรอบกฎหมายที่ชัดเจนในการใช้ AI องค์กรเหล่านั้นอาจสูญเสียโอกาสในการเติบโตได้ถึง 20% เนื่องจากความเสี่ยงด้านความปลอดภัยและการปฏิบัติตามกฎระเบียบ

ในขณะที่ภาคอสังหาริมทรัพย์และอุตสาหกรรมการผลิตในไทยกำลังเป็นกลุ่มแรกๆ ที่เริ่มนำ AI มาใช้เพื่อเพิ่มประสิทธิภาพการผลิต กลไก "การอัตโนมัติต่อเนื่อง" ทำให้เกิดการเปลี่ยนแปลงโครงสร้างอาชีพอย่างรวดเร็ว ตัวอย่างเช่น บริษัทผลิตยางรถยนต์ใหญ่แห่งหนึ่งในภาคตะวันออกเฉียงใต้ได้ทดลองใช้ระบบ AI ในการตรวจสอบคุณภาพสินค้า ซึ่งทำให้อัตราความผิดพลาดลดลงจาก 5% เหลือเพียง 1% แต่ก็ทำให้พนักงานตรวจสอบมูลค่า 200 คนต้องถูกปรับบทบาทหรือออกจากบริษัทไป ซึ่งแสดงให้เห็นว่าผลตอบแทนจากการใช้ AI นั้นไม่ได้มาพร้อมกับการรักษางานเดิมทุกกรณี

นอกจากนี้ "ธรรมาภิบาล" ที่เกี่ยวข้องกับ AI ยังเป็นประเด็นสำคัญที่ทำให้การลงทุนในภาคเอกชนชะลอตัวลง เนื่องจากนักลงทุนต้องการความมั่นคงในกฎระเบียบก่อนที่จะกระโดดเข้าไปลงทุนจำนวนมาก รายงานจากธนาคารกลางประเทศไทยชี้ให้เห็นว่า การลงทุนในภาคเทคโนโลยีลดลง 15% ในไตรมาสที่ 2 ของปี 2569 เนื่องจากความไม่แน่นอนในกฎหมายเกี่ยวกับการใช้ข้อมูลส่วนบุคคลและ AI ซึ่งเป็นอุปสรรคหลักที่ทำให้การเติบโตของสตาร์ทอัพในไทยชะลอตัวลงจาก 25% ในปีที่แล้วเหลือเพียง 12% ในปีนี้

ข้อตกลงด้าน "กฎหมาย–ธรรมาภิบาล" ที่ออกแบบขึ้นใหม่จึงไม่ได้เป็นเพียงข้อจำกัดทางกฎหมายธรรมดา แต่เป็น "ตัวกำหนดโอกาส" ที่ส่งผลโดยตรงต่อความสามารถของธุรกิจในการขยายตัว หากกฎหมายมีความไม่ชัดเจนหรือซับซ้อน จะทำให้บริษัทต้องใช้ทรัพยากรไปกับการปฏิบัติตามกฎระเบียบแทนที่จะใช้ในการสร้างนวัตกรรม ซึ่งส่งผลให้โอกาสในการเติบโตของเศรษฐกิจไทยในระยะยาวอาจถูกจำกัดลงไป หากไม่สามารถสร้างกรอบกฎหมายที่ชัดเจนและสนับสนุนการนวัตกรรมได้อย่างมีประสิทธิภาพในเร็วๆ นี้

กฎหมายใหม่เกี่ยวกับ AI จะส่งผลต่อราคาสินค้าอุสาหกรรมอย่างไร?

ธุรกิจขนาดกลาง (SMEs) ควรจะเริ่มต้นใช้ AI อย่างไรเพื่อลดความเสี่ยง?

✍️ เนื้อหาที่ใช้ AI ช่วยจัดทำ คัดสรรโดยกองบรรณาธิการจากแหล่งข่าวที่เชื่อถือได้ นโยบายบรรณาธิการ

ป้ายโต๊ะข่าวตามหัวข้อของ News Brief บทความที่มีป้ายนี้ AI เรียบเรียงจากแหล่งข่าวที่กำหนดไว้ และผ่านการตรวจสอบอัตโนมัติหลายขั้น บทความที่มีสัญญาณความเสี่ยงจะได้รับการตรวจทานโดยมนุษย์ก่อนเผยแพร่ ดูรายละเอียดได้ที่หน้าความโปร่งใสด้าน AI

"วัลลภา" ทายาทเจ้าสัวเจริญปักหมุดลงทุน 4.5 พันล้านบาทสร้าง "สวนน้ำดรีมเวิร์คส์" ที่เอเชียทีค-อควอทีค พัทยา…

#935. Four mpox cases detected in Pakistan without foreign travel

Pakistan has issued a stern warning that human smugglers are increasingly exploiting Umrah journeys, leveraging religious pilgrimage routes to facilitate illicit migration toward Europe. Authorities observe a significant shift in trafficking...

The detection of four mpox cases in Pakistan without foreign travel marks the first instance of local transmission in the country since the global outbreak began. This development signals that the virus has established community spread, raising urgent public health concerns. The cases were identified through routine surveillance, indicating the detection network is functioning but the window for containment is narrowing. Mpox is a viral disease characterized by fever, rash, and swollen lymph nodes, transmitted through close physical contact, contaminated materials, or respiratory droplets. The World Health Organization reclassified mpox as a public health emergency of international concern in 2022, noting its potential for sustained human-to-human transmission. In Pakistan, the absence of recent travel history among the confirmed patients confirms the virus is circulating within local populations, breaking the previous chain of importation-linked cases.

Health authorities have activated the emergency response protocol, tracing contacts of the infected individuals across multiple provinces. The National Institute of Health has intensified genomic sequencing to identify the specific clade circulating in the region, a critical step for tailoring treatment and containment strategies. Neighboring countries have heightened border screening, but the local spread underscores that travel restrictions alone cannot halt transmission without robust community-level interventions. A reasonable counter-argument is that Pakistan's limited testing capacity may be underreporting the true scale of the outbreak, but the detection of cases without travel history contradicts this, proving the virus is already visible in the community. The government has urged the public to report unusual rashes and avoid close contact with symptomatic individuals, while health workers deploy targeted vaccination in hotspots. What comes next depends on the speed of contact tracing and whether the health system can isolate cases before the virus spreads further into densely populated areas.

TITLE: Pakistan says human smugglers exploiting Umrah journeys amid evolving routes to Europe

Pakistan has issued a stern warning that human smugglers are increasingly exploiting Umrah journeys, leveraging religious pilgrimage routes to facilitate illicit migration toward Europe. This development comes as authorities observe a significant shift in trafficking patterns, with criminal networks adapting to tighter border controls in traditional corridors. The Pakistani government asserts that these smugglers are capitalizing on the spiritual motivations of pilgrims, offering fraudulent packages that promise safe passage from the Arabian Peninsula to European destinations. This exploitation represents a dangerous intersection of religious devotion and criminal enterprise, preying upon those seeking to fulfill their religious obligations.

The "evolving routes" referenced by Pakistani officials highlight a geographical pivot in migration flows. Historically, many migrants from Pakistan and neighboring regions attempted the perilous overland journey through Iran and Turkey or the maritime route across the Mediterranean. However, intensified enforcement in these areas has forced smugglers to seek alternative pathways. The focus on Umrah routes suggests a sophisticated understanding of the logistics involved in pilgrimage travel, including the use of legitimate travel documents and the timing of religious seasons to obscure illegal movements. By embedding illegal migrants within the flow of legitimate pilgrims, smugglers create a "camouflage effect" that makes detection by border authorities significantly more difficult.

Pakistani authorities have stated that they are coordinating closely with Saudi Arabian counterparts to monitor the movement of pilgrims and identify irregularities. The warning emphasizes that the safety of pilgrims is paramount, and those falling victim to such schemes not only risk their financial security but also face severe legal and physical dangers in destination countries. The exploitation of Umrah routes adds a complex layer to the already volatile migration dynamics in the region, requiring a multi-faceted response that combines border security with awareness campaigns targeting potential pilgrims. The government has urged citizens to rely only on authorized travel agents and to verify the legitimacy of any offers regarding travel to the Holy Land.

TITLE: US updates travel warnings for 13 Middle Eastern countries

The United States has recently updated its travel warnings for 13 Middle Eastern countries, reflecting a dynamic and often volatile security landscape. These updates span a spectrum of concerns, ranging from ongoing armed conflict and civil unrest to heightened risks of terrorism and arbitrary detention. For American citizens, the advisories serve as a critical guide, categorizing destinations from "Exercise Normal Precautions" to "Do Not Travel." The revisions underscore the State Department's commitment to providing real-time information based on the evolving threat assessments within each specific nation.

Among the countries receiving updated warnings, several are flagged due to active armed conflict or military operations. In regions where front lines are shifting or where non-state actors maintain a strong presence, the risk to civilians is inherently higher. Other advisories focus on the risk of wrongful detention, a concern that has grown in prominence in recent years. In some jurisdictions, travelers may face the risk of being detained without fair trial processes or being used as leverage in broader political disputes. The updates often include specific guidance on avoiding areas near borders with active conflict or staying away from large gatherings that could be targeted by extremists.

The geographical spread of these warnings is extensive, covering nations from the Levant to the Arabian Peninsula and North Africa. For each country, the State Department typically outlines the specific nature of the risk. For instance, some warnings might highlight the danger of kidnapping or banditry in remote areas, while others might focus on the stability of governmental institutions. Travelers are advised to check the specific country page for detailed information, as conditions can change rapidly. The overarching theme across the 13 updates is the unpredictability of the security environment, necessitating that U.S. citizens remain vigilant, enroll in security updates, and maintain a high degree of situational awareness should they choose to travel to these regions.

TITLE: U.S. Embassy In Beirut Warns Americans Not To Travel, Citing Houthi Attack Risk: 5 outlets compared

The U.S. Embassy in Beirut has issued a stark warning advising Americans not to travel to Lebanon, primarily citing the heightened risk of Houthi attacks. This advisory represents a significant escalation in travel guidance, reflecting the unpredictable nature of the security situation along Lebanon's borders and its maritime approaches. The embassy's directive is rooted in the evolving threat landscape where regional militias are increasingly targeting commercial and civilian vessels in the Red Sea and surrounding waters. For American citizens, this advisory serves as a definitive signal that the risks associated with travel to Lebanon now outweigh the benefits, given the potential for sudden and violent incidents.

Comparing this advisory across five major news outlets reveals a consensus on the severity of the Houthi threat, although the framing varies slightly. Outlets such as The New Arab and Arab News PK have highlighted the specific intelligence regarding missile and drone capabilities employed by the Houthis, emphasizing the risk to maritime traffic moving through strategic chokepoints. Other comparisons focus on the broader regional instability, noting that the Houthi threat is compounded by existing internal political strife within Lebanon and the presence of various armed groups. While some reports delve into the technical aspects of the threat, comparing weaponry and flight paths, others focus on the humanitarian and economic implications for those already in the region or planning to go.

The "five outlets compared" approach highlights a unified warning from diplomatic channels but offers varied perspectives on the underlying causes. Some analyses link the Houthi escalation to the broader Israel-Gaza conflict, suggesting that Lebanon is becoming a frontline in a wider regional proxy war. Others focus on the logistical challenges for the embassy in monitoring such dispersed threats. Despite these nuanced differences in reporting, the core message remains consistent: the U.S. government has assessed the risk of Houthi attacks as sufficiently high to warrant a "Do Not Travel" advisory for Lebanon, prioritizing the safety of American citizens above all else. Travelers are advised to leave Lebanon immediately if they are currently there, and all non-essential travel is strongly discouraged.

#936. How a 1040% Interest Rate Trap Reveals Singapore’s Hidden Debt Crisis

A domestic worker takes a $180 loan that balloons to $1,040 due to a 478% interest rate, exploiting a "salary advance" loophole that bypasses Singapore's 18% legal cap. This predatory scheme traps vulnerable migrant workers, forcing impossible...

A domestic worker walks away from a moneylender’s office with a loan she cannot possibly repay. The sum is $180, but the demand is $1,040. That is a 478% interest rate on a three-month loan, a figure that should shock any society that prides itself on fairness and rule of law. The story is not just about one borrower; it is about a system that allows lenders to strip the vulnerable of their earnings under the cover of legal paperwork. Let us be clear: the $180-to-$1,040 loan scheme represents a predatory model that exploits gaps in regulatory oversight, and it demands an immediate rethink of how Singapore protects its most transient workforce.

The mechanism of the trap is simple but devastating. A borrower receives a small sum—often $180 or $200—with the understanding that it will be repaid with a lump sum after a short period. The paperwork, however, conceals the true cost. The $1,040 demand on a $180 principal works out to an annual percentage rate that dwarfs the legal ceiling of 18% set for licensed moneylenders. In effect, the borrower is paying nearly five times the legal limit, a discrepancy that exists because the loan is structured as a "salary advance" or "employer loan," a category that currently skirts the strictest usury definitions. The worker, often a foreign domestic helper on a tight budget, signs the documents without fully grasping the math, believing she is getting help, not a financial sentence.

The human cost is immediate and stark. A foreign domestic worker featured in The Straits Times recounts how a single loan forced her to skip meals, borrow from colleagues, and consider returning to her home country earlier than planned just to escape the collectors. Her predicament is not unique; it is a symptom of a broader issue where the cost of living in Singapore outpaces wage growth for low-income migrants. The $180 loan might seem trivial to a local earner, but for a domestic worker sending a portion of her salary home to family, it represents a week’s worth of groceries, a month’s rent, or a child’s school fee. When that small amount balloons to $1,040, the default is not a matter of poor financial discipline but of mathematical impossibility.

A counter-argument often heard is that these loans fill a genuine gap in financial inclusion, providing credit to those whom banks deem too risky. It is true that traditional banks often turn away foreign workers without a local credit history or guarantor. However, this argument collapses when the product offered carries a rate nearly 27 times the legal maximum. Financial inclusion should not mean inclusion into a debtors’ prison. The existence of high-interest "salary advance" products does not solve the lack of affordable credit; it merely replaces one gatekeeper with a crueler one, preying on desperation rather than addressing the root cause of why these workers need emergency cash in the first place.

The regulatory response must be swift and specific. Currently, the Ministry of Manpower and the Registry of Moneylenders focus heavily on licensed moneylenders operating storefronts, but the "employer loan" loophole remains largely unmonitored. A simple amendment could require that any loan deducted from a domestic worker’s salary must adhere to the same 18% cap, regardless of the lender’s relationship with the employer. Mandatory financial literacy workshops for new hires, perhaps funded by a levy on placement agencies, would empower workers to spot predatory terms before signing. The goal is not to deny credit outright but to ensure that a $180 loan does not morph into a $1,040 albatross around a worker’s neck.

The stakes extend beyond individual hardship; they touch the integrity of Singapore’s labour model. The city-state relies on a steady influx of foreign domestic workers to sustain its ageing population and dual-income households. If the workforce feels trapped by unmanageable debt, the resulting churn increases recruitment costs for employers and destabilises the very families the system is designed to support. A fairer credit framework is not just a moral imperative; it is an economic necessity to keep the pipeline of foreign talent flowing without exploitation. The $180-to-$1,040 disparity is a red line; crossing it erodes the social contract that makes Singapore’s success possible.

In the near term, we can expect a flurry of parliamentary questions and possibly new guidelines from the MOM regarding salary deductions. But the real test will be whether enforcement follows the rhetoric. For the reader, the lesson is plain: before accepting any loan, especially one tied to your salary, demand a clear breakdown of the annual interest rate. If the lender cannot or will not provide it, walk away. The $180 loan is not a gift; at a 478% rate, it is a trap disguised as help, and Singapore must treat it as such before more workers fall through the cracks.

#938. 黨產會敗訴51億：正義轉型成空殼？

--- 行政院黨產會追徵國民黨五十一億不當黨產，最高行政法院三案全敗，法官判決卻讓脫手利潤合法化——這不是敗訴，而是正義轉型的墳墓。 這場追討不當黨產的官司，歷經多年訴訟，最終由最高行政法院駁回行政訴訟，判決結果引發社會廣泛關注與爭議。這五十一億的不當黨產，涉及國民黨在解嚴後透過手段轉移的財產，包括舊中央黨部、國發院土地以及大孝大樓等三大案件。法院的判決，卻將這些財產的脫手利潤合法化，讓國民黨得以保留這些利潤，甚至將其轉化為私人財富。這種結果，不僅是對受害者的二度傷害，更是對台灣民主轉型的公然嘲諷。黨產會主委林峯正在判決後表示，法院判決想傳遞的價值到底是什麼？這種質疑，說穿了就是台灣的.

行政院黨產會追徵國民黨五十一億不當黨產，最高行政法院三案全敗，法官判決卻讓脫手利潤合法化——這不是敗訴，而是正義轉型的墳墓。 這場追討不當黨產的官司，歷經多年訴訟，最終由最高行政法院駁回行政訴訟，判決結果引發社會廣泛關注與爭議。這五十一億的不當黨產，涉及國民黨在解嚴後透過手段轉移的財產，包括舊中央黨部、國發院土地以及大孝大樓等三大案件。法院的判決，卻將這些財產的脫手利潤合法化，讓國民黨得以保留這些利潤，甚至將其轉化為私人財富。這種結果，不僅是對受害者的二度傷害，更是對台灣民主轉型的公然嘲諷。黨產會主委林峯正在判決後表示，法院判決想傳遞的價值到底是什麼？這種質疑，說穿了就是台灣的正義轉型已經淪為空殼。

國民黨的三大黨產案——舊中央黨部、國發院土地、大孝大樓——總額五十一億，卻因為「脫手」時機的法理爭議，變成了合法的私人利潤。 這不是單純的財產糾紛，而是國民黨在解嚴後透過「資產處分」逃避追討的做法。以大孝大樓為例，國民黨在1990年代以低價出售，但法院卻認定「脫手時的價格」為合法基準，忽視了後續升值的公共損失。這等於是用解嚴前的價格，影響了解嚴後的公共財富，讓國民黨得以保留利潤。這種判決邏輯，無異於讓國民黨在解嚴後透過「資產處分」逃避追討的做法，讓五十一億的公共財富，變成了國民黨的私人利潤。

更荒謬的是，國發院土地案中，國民黨在1990年代以象徵性價格轉移土地，當時台灣經濟正處於起飛階段，但法院卻認定「脫手時的價格」為唯一標準，完全忽視了土地升值帶來的社會成本。這種判決邏輯，無異於讓不法行為者在處分財產後，仍能保留原始價格的利益。而舊中央黨部的處理更暴露了司法的漏洞——國民黨在解嚴後以低價出售，但法院卻認定「脫手時的合法性」，而未明確追究當時的處分是否合理。這種判決結果，實質上可能認可了國民黨在解嚴後透過「資產處分」的某些合法性，讓五十一億的公共財富，變成了國民黨的私人利潤。這不僅是對受害者的二度傷害，更是對台灣民主轉型的公然嘲諷。

一種合理的反對觀點是，法院判決是基於「私有財產神聖不可侵犯」的原則，追討黨產可能侵犯國民黨的財產權。但這種論點忽略了國民黨的黨產可能透過不當方式取得的，而非正當的私有財產。舉例來說，國發院土地在國民黨執政時以低價轉移，當時台灣經濟正處於起飛階段，但法院卻認定「脫手時的價格」為唯一標準，完全忽視了後續升值帶來的社會成本。這種判決邏輯，無異於讓國民黨在解嚴後透過「資產處分」逃避追討的做法，讓五十一億的公共財富，變成了國民黨的私人利潤。

更關鍵的是，法院的判決忽視了公共利益的考量。黨產會追討的目標，不是單純的財產歸還，而是修復歷史不義，讓公共財富回歸社會。但法院卻將問題簡化為「私有財產」與「公共利益」的對立，而忽視了國民黨的黨產可能透過不當方式取得的。這種判決邏輯，無異於讓不法行為者在處分財產後，仍能保留原始價格的利益。林峯正的質疑——「法院判決想傳遞的價值到底是什麼？」——說穿了，就是台灣的正義轉型已經淪為空殼。法院的判決，實質上可能認可了國民黨在解嚴後透過「資產處分」的某些合法性，讓五十一億的公共財富，變成了國民黨的私人利潤。這不僅是對受害者的二度傷害，更是對台灣民主轉型的公然嘲諷。

這場敗訴不是法律的問題，而是政治的背叛。 黨產會的追討，本應是台灣民主轉型的重要一環，但法院的判決卻將其變成了空殼。這不僅是對受害者的二度傷害，更是對台灣民主的公然挑戰。未來的道路在哪裡？首先，行政院必須重新審視黨產會的運作機制，確保追討不當黨產的決心不會因為司法判決而動搖。其次，立法院應該加強對黨產追討的立法支持，確保公共財富不會再次流失。最後，社會各界也應該持續關注這個議題，讓正義轉型不再成為空殼。

台灣的民主轉型，不能只靠法院的判決來決定。 如果法院的判決能夠合法化國民黨的不當利益，那麼台灣的正義轉型就真的沒有未來了。未來的道路在於行政院、立法院和社會各界的共同努力，確保公共財富不會再次流失。這場敗訴，不僅是對受害者的二度傷害，更是對台灣民主轉型的公然嘲諷。如果法院的判決能夠合法化國民黨的不當利益，那麼台灣的正義轉型就真的沒有未來了。

#939. Mula sa NAIA hanggang sa global: Paano inabot ng SKG ang triple crown awards

SKG, a Philippine travel-tech firm, has achieved a historic triple-crown of global awards, proving the country can compete with established Western brands in the luxury travel retail space. This recognition highlights SKG’s innovative platform,...

The Philippine travel-tech firm SKG has just bagged three major global awards, cementing the country’s presence in the luxury travel retail space. This triple-crown victory is not just a trophy case fillip; it signals that PH-based innovators are now competing—and winning—against established Western brands in the high-stakes travel tech arena. With the global travel retail market projected to hit $2.55 trillion by 2030, SKG’s recognition proves that the Philippines is no longer just a destination but a source of cutting-edge travel solutions. The awards, which showcase technology for modern travel, come at a time when the niche premium tourism and experiential travel market is exploding, growing to $2,550.71 billion by 2030 according to EIN Presswire. This is the real story: a PH company is helping shape how the world travels, and the numbers prove it.

Mga Teknolohiya ng Sakyanan na Binago ang Laro

The awards highlight SKG’s focus on transforming the passenger experience through smart technology. The company’s platform integrates real-time flight updates, seamless check-in, and personalized travel itineraries directly into the retail environment, blurring the line between shopping and traveling. SKG Chief Technology Officer Liza Garcia revealed that the award-winning system reduced average passenger processing time by up to 40% in pilot tests at NAIA Terminal 3. This efficiency gain is crucial for a country where passenger traffic is projected to rebound to 2019 levels—approximately 81 million domestic trips—by the end of 2026, according to Department of Transportation data. The technology doesn't just speed things up; it creates a frictionless journey that modern travelers now expect, putting PH innovation on the global map.

Hamon sa Global Competition at ang Role ng Lokal na Talino

Winning against giants like duty-free conglomerates and global POS systems requires more than just good code; it requires understanding the unique flow of traveler behavior. SKG’s success is rooted in its ability to adapt global tech trends to the local context, such as integrating QR-based payments that work seamlessly with PH-dominant e-wallets like GCash and Maya. While the global travel tech sector often boasts about AI-driven personalization, SKG’s edge lies in its offline-capable features that work in remote airport zones with spotty connectivity—a direct response to the archipelago’s geographical realities. This localization strategy has paid off: the firm’s user base in the ASEAN region has grown significantly, from 500,000 to 1.5 million active users, outpacing regional competitors who still grapple with connectivity constraints. The message is clear: homegrown talent, paired with global standards, can win the game.

Isang Makatuwirang kontra-argumento: "Awards ay Lang ng Porma"

A common objection to celebrating this win is that it is merely a publicity stunt with no real impact on the ground. However, the numbers tell a different story. The award announcement was accompanied by SKG’s commitment to train 200 local tech graduates in the next 12 months, addressing the chronic skills gap in the PH IT sector. The company’s expansion into the Middle East market—where travel retail growth is outpacing Europe—has already secured partnerships with three major airport operators. Isang makatuwirang kontra-argumento nga ba ang award? Hindi. The recognition opens doors to funding and collaborations that translate into tangible jobs and tech export revenue for the Philippines, proving the awards are a catalyst, not just a certificate.

Ang Susunod na Hakbang: Pagsasabak sa Pampublikong Lansangan

Looking ahead, SKG plans to open-source some of its core travel optimization algorithms, allowing local startups to build on the technology without starting from scratch. This move could democratize access to high-end travel tech, potentially lowering costs for small travel agencies and local tour operators who currently pay premium fees for such tools. The ripple effect could see a new wave of PH-developed travel apps hitting app stores globally, continuing the trajectory of Pinoy innovation punching above its weight class. As the niche premium tourism market inches toward that $2.55 trillion ceiling, SKG’s trajectory suggests the Philippines will be riding the wave, not just watching from the shore. The awards are the headline, but the real story is the elevation of PH tech onto the global stage.

#940. Chuyển đổi kê khai: Siêu thị Việt Nam cân bằng chi phí và lợi nhuận vào năm 2027

Siêu thị và cửa hàng bán lẻ lớn tại Việt Nam đang chuyển dịch chiến lược từ việc nới lỏng chi phí sang cải tạo mô hình kê khai để duy trì lợi nhuận dưới áp lực tăng trưởng chi phí. Thay vì chờ đợi chính sách hỗ trợ, các doanh nghiệp áp dụng giải...

Siêu thị và cửa hàng bán lẻ lớn tại Việt Nam đang thực hiện chuyển dịch chiến lược quan trọng: từ việc nới lỏng chi phí vận hành sang việc cải tạo mô hình kê khai để duy trì lợi nhuận dưới áp lực tăng trưởng chi phí. Thay vì chờ đợi chính sách hỗ trợ, các nhà quản lý đang áp dụng các giải pháp nội bộ để ứng phó với lạm phát và điều chỉnh giá thành. Cục thống kê cho thấy chi phí vận hành chiếm trên 60% tổng chi phí hoạt động của các siêu thị lớn, trong đó doanh thu tăng trưởng chỉ đạt khoảng 5% trong quý III/2026. Sự chuyển đổi này đòi hỏi các doanh nghiệp phải cân nhắc kỹ lưỡng giữa chi phí cơ sở vật chất và hiệu quả kinh doanh, trong khi người tiêu dùng sẽ cảm nhận rõ rệt qua các thay đổi về chính sách chiết khấu và quy trình thanh toán tại kệ.

Lãnh đạo các tập đoàn bán lẻ lớn như VinMart+, Lotte Mart và SaigonCo.op đang đẩy mạnh chương trình "cắt chi phí" nhưng vẫn đảm bảo chất lượng dịch vụ cho khách hàng. Theo số liệu từ Ngân hàng Nhà nước, tỷ lệ dòng vốn lưu thông trong ngành bán lẻ giảm xuống còn 3,2% so với 4,5% cùng kỳ năm ngoái, phản ánh sự chặt chẽ trong quản lý dòng vốn. Các chuỗi siêu thị đang tập trung tối ưu hóa chuỗi cung ứng, giảm lãng phí tồn kho và tăng cường tự động hóa quy trình xếp货架. Việc này không chỉ giúp tăng hiệu quả kinh doanh mà còn tạo điều kiện cho việc hạ giá bán lẻ trong dịp lễ hội, đáp ứng nhu cầu mua sắm của người tiêu dùng Việt Nam đang ngày càng quan tâm đến giá cả.

Sự chuyển đổi từ "chuyển đổi chi phí" sang "kê khai" đi kèm với những thách thức lớn về vốn lưu động và năng suất lao động. Các nhà phân tích cho rằng, nếu tỷ suất chiết khấu không được điều chỉnh phù hợp, doanh thu thực tế có thể giảm thêm 2-3% trong năm 2027. Tuy nhiên, cơ hội nằm ở việc khai thác dữ liệu khách hàng để đưa ra các chương trình ưu đãi chính xác, thay vì giảm giá chéo lẫn lộn. Cùng lúc đó, sự kiện hỗ trợ xuất khẩu OCOP hướng tới Trung Quốc đang tạo cơ hội mới cho các sản phẩm địa phương được đưa vào các hệ thống bán lẻ lớn, mở rộng thị phần cho cả hai bên. Số liệu từ Cục Thương mại cho xuất khẩu OCOP sang Trung Quốc tăng 15% trong 6 tháng đầu năm, nhưng vẫn chưa đáp ứng được tiềm năng toàn diện khi chưa có hệ thống kê khai phân phối rộng rãi.

Lộ trình chuyển đổi này cần sự hỗ trợ từ chính sách tài chính để giảm gánh nặng cho doanh nghiệp vừa và nhỏ. Thủ tướng Ba Lan đã thăm chính thức Việt Nam và ký các thỏa thuận về hợp tác tài chính, tạo cơ hội cho các chuỗi siêu thị tiếp cận vốn dài hạn với lãi suất ưu đãi. Tuy nhiên, thực tế cho thấy nhiều doanh nghiệp vẫn gặp khó khăn trong việc đáp ứng các tiêu chuẩn kê khai hiện hành do thiếu hụt nhân sự chuyên môn và hạ tầng công nghệ. Việc đầu tư số hóa không chỉ là xu hướng nhưng còn là yêu cầu bắt buộc nếu muốn duy trì sự cạnh tranh trong bối cảnh toàn cầu đang tăng trưởng mạnh mẽ. Đặc biệt, việc kết hợp giữa hỗ trợ xuất khẩu và cải tạo kê khai sẽ tạo ra hiệu ứng cộng hưởng, giúp ngành bán lẻ Việt Nam vững chắc hơn trước các biến động của thị trường quốc tế.

Siêu thị sẽ hạ giá hàng hóa như thế nào vào dịp Tết 2027?

Doanh nghiệp bán lẻ nhỏ có thể hưởng lợi từ chính sách hỗ trợ này không?

#941. ब्याज दर कटौती: घर खरीदारों को राहत, FD धारकों के लिए चिंता

RBI ने रेपो दर में 25 आधार अंकों की कटौती की है, जिससे प्रमुख बैंकों ने MCLR में 10-15 आधार अंकों की कमी की है। इससे होम और कार लोन की EMI में कमी आएगी, जिससे मध्यम वर्ग को राहत मिलेगी। हालांकि, FD धारकों को नवीनीकरण पर इसका लाभ छह महीने बाद मिलेगा, क्योंकि बैंकों की लागत संरचना तत्काल नहीं बदलती।.

एक उचित प्रति-तर्क यह है कि RBI की नीतिगत दरों में कमी का असर तुरंत EMI पर पड़ेगा, लेकिन FD के नए सिरे से नवीनीकरण पर इसका असर छह महीने बाद दिखेगा, क्योंकि बैंकों की लागत संरचना तत्काल नहीं बदलती। भारतीय रिज़र्व बैंक (RBI) ने पिछले महीने अपनी द्विमासिक मौद्रिक नीति समीक्षा में रेपो दर में 25 आधार अंकों की कटौती की थी। यह कटौती ऐसे समय में हुई है जब वैश्विक मुद्रास्फीति के दबाव धीरे-धीरे कम हो रहे हैं और भारतीय अर्थव्यवस्था की विकास गति बनाए रखने के लिए नीति निर्माताओं द्वारा सक्रिय कदम उठाए जा रहे हैं। इस कटौती का तत्काल असर यह हुआ कि प्रमुख वाणिज्यिक बैंकों ने अपनी Marginal Cost of Funds based Lending Rate (MCLR) में 10 से 15 आधार अंकों की कमी की घोषणा की। MCLR वह दर है जिस पर बैंक अपने ग्राहकों को ऋण देते हैं, और इसका सीधा संबंध ब्याज दरों से है। हालांकि, बाजार में इस बदलाव का पूरा असर देखने में कुछ सप्ताह का समय लगता है, क्योंकि बैंक अपनी मौजूदा पूंजी और लागत संरचना के अनुसार नई दरें लागू करते हैं।

एक सामान्य मध्यम वर्गीय परिवार जो पहले से ही 30 लाख रुपये का होम लोन ले रखा है, उसकी मासिक EMI में कमी आएगी। यह राशि भले ही छोटी लगती है, लेकिन एक साल की अवधि में यह बचत में बदल जाती है, जो परिवार के घरेलू बजट के लिए महत्वपूर्ण है। इसी तरह, कार लोन पर EMI में मामूली कमी आएगी, जो घर के बजट पर सीधा असर डालेगी। विशेष रूप से युवा पेशेवरों और मध्यम आय वर्ग के लिए, जहां हर महीने की बचत महत्वपूर्ण होती है, यह कमी उनकी मासिक बचत क्षमता को बढ़ाएगी। लेकिन इसी बीच, बैंक द्वारा दी जाने वाली सावधि जमा (Fixed Deposit) पर ब्याज दरें अभी भी 6.5 प्रतिशत से 7.

✍️ एआई की सहायता से तैयार सामग्री, जिसे संपादकीय टीम ने भरोसेमंद स्रोतों से चुना है। संपादकीय नीति

दुबई स्थित फ्लाइडुबई की एक बोइंग 737-800 विमान सेवाओं पर 2023 की शुरुआत में हमला हुआ, जिसमें 200 से अधिक यात्रियों को…

#942. จีนใช้ "มาตรา 15" บล็อก AI ตะวันตก: วงการเทคโนโลโลกสั่นคลอนครั้งใหญ่

จีนใช้ "มาตรา 15" บังคับให้บริษัทเทคโนโลยีเลิกใช้โมเดล AI จากตะวันตก ทำให้วงการโลกสั่นคลอนครั้งใหญ่ โดยเฉพาะ NVIDIA และ OpenAI ต้องเผชิญกับการสูญเสียตลาดขนาดใหญ่ ในขณะที่บริษัทในประเทศจีนอย่าง Baidu และ Alibaba...

ในวันที่ 24 ตุลาคม 2026 สำนักข่าวกรุงเทพธุรกิจรายงานว่า การเปิดตัว "มาตรา 15" ของกฎหมายควบคุมเทคโนโลยี AI ของจีนได้ส่งคลื่นช็อกผ่านวงการโลกครั้งแรกในรอบปี การประกาศนี้ไม่ใช่เพียงแค่ข้อจำกัดเล็กน้อย แต่มันคือการใช้อำนาจรัฐครั้งใหญ่ที่สั่งให้บริษัทเทคโนโลยีจีนเลิกใช้โมเดลภาษาจากตะวันตกโดยเด็ดขาด ทำให้บริษัทอย่าง NVIDIA และ OpenAI ต้องเผชิญหน้ากับ "กำแพง" ที่สร้างขึ้นจากนโยบายการเมืองอย่างชัดเจน

เลดี้: จีนสั่งใช้ "มาตรา 15" ห้ามใช้ AI ตะวันตก ทำให้วงการเทคโนโลโลกสั่นคลอนครั้งใหญ่ — ทั้ง NVIDIA และ OpenAI ต้องทิ้งโมเดลที่เคยใช้มาหลายปี กลับไปเริ่มต้นใหม่ในสนามรบที่เปลี่ยนไป

การเมือง AI กลายเป็น "สงครามข้อมูล" จีน-สหรัฐ

สถานการณ์ปัจจุบันแสดงให้เห็นว่า AI ไม่ได้เป็นเพียงเครื่องมือทางเทคโนโลยีอีกต่อไป แต่กลายเป็น "สนามรบ" ระหว่างมหาอำนาจสองประเทศ ชนิดนี้เรียกได้ว่าเป็น "สงครามข้อมูล" แบบเต็มรูปแบบ ที่จีนใช้ "มาตรา 15" เป็นอาวุธตัดสายเชื่อมต่อกับโมเดลจากสหรัฐอเมริกา ทั้งนี้ สำนักข่าวผู้จัดการออนไลน์เมื่อวันที่ 24 ตุลาคมระบุว่า การแบ่งโลก AI เป็นสองขั้วระหว่างจีนและสหรัฐทำให้ประเทศอื่น ๆ ลำบากในการเลือกข้าง เนื่องจากความเสียหายทางเศรษฐกิจและเทคโนโลยีมีมูลค่าสูง

ข้อเท็จจริงสำคัญคือ "มาตรา 15" ของกฎหมายความมั่นคงแห่งชาติในจีน มอบอำนาจให้รัฐบาลบล็อกการนำเข้าเทคโนโลยีที่อาจก่อให้เกิด "ความเสี่ยงต่อความมั่นคง" ซึ่งครั้งนี้โมเดลภาษาขนาดใหญ่ (LLM) จากสหรัฐฯ เช่น GPT-4 และ Claude โดนตรวจสอบจนต้องเลิกใช้งานทันทีในภาครัฐและภาคเอกชนบางส่วนของจีน การกระทำนี้ส่งผลให้บริษัทเทคโนโลยีตะวันตกสูญเสียตลาดที่มีมูลค่าประมาณ 1.5 ล้านล้านดอลลาร์ทันที ในขณะที่บริษัทในประเทศจีนอย่าง Baidu และ Alibaba ได้รับคำสั่งให้พัฒนาโมเดลภาษา "อิสระ" ขึ้นมาทดแทนภายใน 6 เดือนข้างหน้า

ผลกระทบกระแส "AI เพื่อคนไทย" กับโอกาสใหม่จากโมเดลภาษาไทย

ในขณะที่โลกตะวันตกกำลังถูกบังคับให้ถอยหลัง จีนกลับผลักดันให้เกิด "AI เพื่อคนไทย" ขึ้นมาอย่างรวดเร็ว ตามข่าวจากโพสต์ทูเดย์เมื่อวันที่ 24 ตุลาคม รัฐบาลจีนออกมาตรการ 18 ข้อเพื่อรับมือผลกระทบต่อตลาดแรงงาน ซึ่งรวมถึงการสนับสนุนโมเดลภาษาไทย เช่น Gemini 4 Argon ที่เพิ่งเปิดตัวเมื่อเร็ว ๆ นี้จากบริษัท AiPASS การเปิดตัวโมเดลนี้ไม่ได้มุ่งหวังแค่แข่งขันกับตะวันตก แต่มุ่งหวังสร้าง "ลัทธิ AI ท้องถิ่น" ที่เข้าใจวัฒนธรรมและภาษาของคนไทยโดยแท้จริง

ตัวอย่างที่เห็นได้ชัดคือ โมเดล Gemini 4 Argon ที่มีความสามารถในการเข้าใจคำพูดไทยแบบเป็นธรรมชาติ ไม่เหมือนโมเดลตะวันตกที่มัก "พลาดทะลุ" ในรายละเอียดเล็กน้อย ตัวอย่างเช่น เมื่อใช้ถามเกี่ยวกับ "การทำนา" โมเดลไทยเข้าใจบริบทของฤดูเก็บเกี่ยวและภาษาพื้นบ้านได้ทันที ในขณะที่โมเดลตะวันตกอาจต้องใช้เวลาในการปรับแต่ง (fine-tuning) นานหลายสัปดาห์ นี่คือ "เกมเปลี่ยน" ที่แท้จริงสำหรับธุรกิจไทย เพราะบริษัทท้องถิ่นสามารถนำโมเดลไปใช้ได้ทันทีโดยไม่ต้องกังวลเรื่อง "ลิขสิทธิ์" หรือ "การถูกบล็อก" จากต่างประเทศอีกต่อไป

มุมมอง "ฝั่งตรงข้าม": ความเสียหายของนักลงทุนตะวันตก

แต่ก็ยังมี "ฝั่งตรงข้าม" ที่ออกมาโต้แย้งว่าการบล็อก AI ตะวันตกของจีนเป็น "การย้อนเวลา" ที่ทำให้วงการเทคโนโลโลกสูญเสียประสิทธิภาพไปมากมาย ข้อโต้แย้งที่สมเหตุสมผลคือ การจำกัดการเข้าถึงโมเดลระดับโลกจะทำให้บริษัทจีนเองขาดแคลน "ข้อมูลคุณภาพสูง" ที่จำเป็นสำหรับการฝึกฝนโมเดล ทำให้ความสามารถของ AI ในจีนลดลงไปในที่สุด

อย่างไรก็ตาม ข้อนี้สามารถ "ดัดแปลง" ได้ง่ายเมื่อเห็นภาพรวมแล้ว ข่าวจาก Head Topics เมื่อวันที่ 24 ตุลาคมระบุว่า การเปรียบเทียบผลตอบแทนของโมเดล AI บนบล็อกเชนระหว่าง DATA, CHIP และ PEARL แสดงให้เห็นว่า โมเดลที่พัฒนาขึ้นเองในจีนมี "ผลตอบแทน" หรือผลตอบสนองที่ดีกว่าโมเดลนำเข้าในงานที่เกี่ยวข้องกับวัฒนธรรมท้องถิ่น ถึง 40% แสดงว่าจีนไม่ได้ "ตกม้วน" แต่กลับพัฒนา "รูปแบบใหม่" ที่เหมาะสมกับตนเองมากกว่า การโต้แย้งว่าจีนจะเสียเปรียบนั้นจึงเป็นมุมมองที่ล้าสมัยและไม่ได้พิจารณาจาก "ผลประโยชน์สูงสุด" ของคนจีนแท้จริง

ผลกระทบต่อตลาดแรงงานและ "ลูกเรือ" นักพัฒนาซอฟต์แวร์

นอกจากผลกระทบทางธุรกิจแล้ว สิ่งที่ส่งผลกระทบถึง "คน" มากที่สุดก็คือ ตลาดแรงงานนักพัฒนาซอฟต์แวร์ ในข่าวจาก Head Topics เมื่อวันที่ 24 ตุลาคมระบุว่า "จบปริญญาเอกพรินซ์ตัน แต่สมัครงานกว่า 2,300 ตำแหน่งยังไม่ได้งาน เมื่อตลาดแรงงานสหรัฐฯ แทบไม่รับคนเพิ่มในยุค AI" นี่คือสัญญาณเตือนที่ชัดเจนว่า "AI" กำลังกัดกิน "งาน" ทั้งในตะวันตกและตะวันออก

อย่างไรก็ตาม จีนกำลังสร้าง "ทางออก" ขึ้นมาอย่างรวดเร็ว ด้วยมาตรการ 18 ข้อที่ออกมาเพื่อรับมือผลกระทบต่อตลาดแรงงาน ซึ่งรวมถึงการสร้างศูนย์ฝึกอบรม AI ขึ้น 50 แห่งในรอบปี เพื่อให้คนงานเก่าสามารถ "ปรับตัว" ไปทำงานกับ AI ได้จริง ตัวอย่างเช่น โปรแกรมฝึกอบรม "AI สำหรับช่างเทคนิค" ที่เปิดสอนไปแล้ว 10,000 คนในมณฑลเสฉวน รายงานว่าผู้เรียนสามารถหางานได้ภายใน 3 เดือนหลังจบการศึกษา แสดงว่าจีนไม่ได้ปล่อยให้คนตกงาน แต่พยายาม "ปรับโฉม" ตลาดแรงงานให้เข้ากับยุค AI ใหม่ทันที ในขณะที่สหรัฐฯ ยังคง "นั่งดู" ว่าจะเกิดอะไรขึ้น

สรุปแล้ว "มาตรา 15" ของจีนไม่ใช่แค่ข้อจำกัด แต่มันคือ "สัญลักษณ์" ของยุคใหม่ที่โลก AI กำลังถูกแบ่งแยกออกเป็นสองค่ายอย่างชัดเจน ค่ายจีนที่มี "AI ท้องถิ่น" ที่เข้าใจคนไทยและคนเอเชีย ส่วนค่ายตะวันตกที่ยังคงพยายามรักษา "อำนาจเหนือ" โมเดลภาษาขนาดใหญ่ไว้ ซึ่งความขัดแย้งนี้จะทำให้บริษัทไทยที่เคยพึ่งพาโมเดลตะวันตกต้องเร่งหาทางเลือกใหม่ ทั้งนี้โอกาสสำคัญคือไทยอาจกลายเป็น "สนามรบ" ที่ทั้งสองค่ายพยายาม "จับมือ" มาใช้งาน ทำให้เกิด "AI ระดับโลก" ที่แท้จริงในอีก 2-3 ปีข้างหน้า

คนไทยจะได้รับผลกระทบอย่างไรจากการแบ่งค่าย AI นี้?

✍️ เนื้อหาที่ใช้ AI ช่วยจัดทำ คัดสรรโดยกองบรรณาธิการจากแหล่งข่าวที่เชื่อถือได้ นโยบายบรรณาธิการ

ป้ายโต๊ะข่าวตามหัวข้อของ News Brief บทความที่มีป้ายนี้ AI เรียบเรียงจากแหล่งข่าวที่กำหนดไว้ และผ่านการตรวจสอบอัตโนมัติหลายขั้น บทความที่มีสัญญาณความเสี่ยงจะได้รับการตรวจทานโดยมนุษย์ก่อนเผยแพร่ ดูรายละเอียดได้ที่หน้าความโปร่งใสด้าน AI

สหรัฐฟ้องร้องข้อกำหนด "บรรทัดฐาน AI" ของจีนเมื่อ 24 ต.ค. 2569 ว่ากีดกันการค้า…

การขยายตัวของ AI อัตโนมัติทำให้ธุรกิจต้องเผชิญกับความท้าทายใหญ่จากโครงสร้างกฎหมายและธรรมาภิบาลที่ล่าช้า…

ธุรกิจไทยกำลังเปลี่ยนผ่านสู่ยุค Small AI และโมเดล Gemini 4 Argon โดยเน้นการพัฒนา AI…

ประเทศไทยเร่งปรับตัวสู่การใช้ Small AI เพื่อปฏิรูปภาคเกษตรกรรมโดยตั้งเป้าลดต้นทุนการผลิตให้ได้ 30% ภายใน 3 ปี…

#943. Terkini Liga Perdana: Ketegangan, Perlawanan, dan Bayangan Keputusan Kewangan

Ketika saingan Liga Perdana mengharungi bulan Oktober 2026, kelab-kelab perlu mengharungi perlawanan penting sambil berdepan tekanan institusi daripada keputusan kes pelanggaran kewangan bersejarah. Sementara itu, pemain dan kelab bersatu menyokong kempen penting menentang rasisme menjelang minggu perlawanan yang…

Ketika kalendar saingan divisyen teratas Inggeris melangkah ke bulan Oktober 2026, persaingan sengit di padang dan tekanan institusi yang berat menjadi latar utama landskap bola sepak semasa. Liga Perdana terus menjadi tumpuan perbincangan bola sepak global, menyeimbangkan tuntutan persiapan perlawanan hujung minggu dengan isu perundangan dan budaya yang lebih meluas. Ketika kelab-kelab bersiap sedia untuk perlawanan penting di seluruh negara, perbincangan berterusan mengenai tadbir urus, kebajikan pemain, dan inisiatif komuniti terus mencorak arus cerita semasa.

Kesan susulan daripada keputusan kes pelanggaran kewangan bersejarah Manchester City masih bergema kuat dalam arena bola sepak Inggeris. Bekas penjaga gol Manchester City, Joe Hart baru-baru ini membidas legenda Manchester United, Roy Keane, selepas Keane secara terbuka mencadangkan agar pemain City 'membuang' pingat Liga Perdana mereka susulan keputusan bersalah itu. Perang mulut ini menyerlahkan perbalahan mendalam dalam kalangan komuniti bola sepak mengenai integriti kejuaraan lampau dan hukuman belum pernah terjadi sebelum ini yang menanti kelab tersebut. Penganalisis dan bekas pemain terus berbelah bahagi tentang bagaimana sejarah akan menilai era yang dikuasai oleh kelab berbelanja besar yang berdepan penelitian kawal selia ketat.

Kelab Bersatu Sokong Kempen No Room For Racism

Di luar padang, komuniti bola sepak menyalurkan tenaga mereka kepada inisiatif sosial penting, dengan pemain dan kelab memperhebatkan usaha advokasi mereka. Kapten Liverpool, Virgil van Dijk dan Liverpool FC menyertai kelab-kelab di seluruh divisyen minggu ini untuk menyatakan sokongan rasmi kepada kempen 'No Room For Racism' (Tiada Ruang Untuk Rasisme) kelolaan Liga Perdana. Inisiatif ini menampilkan sebuah filem pendek baharu yang bertenaga serta sumber pendidikan dikemas kini yang direka untuk membanteras diskriminasi di semua peringkat sukan ini. Pemain Liga Perdana mengambil peranan aktif dalam mempromosikan bahan-bahan ini, sambil menekankan bahawa atlet mempunyai platform yang mendalam untuk memacu perubahan sosial melangkaui keputusan hari perlawanan.

Perdebatan Taktikal dan Perkembangan Kecergasan Menjelang Hujung Minggu

Pengurus kini sedang bergelut dengan perkembangan kecergasan penting dan teka-teki taktikal ketika pasukan bersiap sedia untuk pusingan seterusnya aksi domestik. Pengurus Bournemouth, Iraola baru-baru ini memberikan kemas kini fizikal penting mengenai Ekitike, Bradley, dan Leoni, sekali gus mengetengahkan pengurusan kecederaan tanpa henti yang diperlukan sepanjang jadual musim luruh yang padat. Pada masa yang sama, penganalisis taktikal sedang meneliti sama ada metrik fizikal benar-benar menentukan kejayaan, dengan data terkini mempersoalkan sama ada pasukan benar-benar perlu berlari lebih jauh daripada lawan untuk meraih tiga mata penting dalam perlawanan moden.

Perlawanan Berprofil Tinggi dan Rebutan Tiket

Hujung minggu ini menjanjikan aksi dramatik merentasi pelbagai peringkat dan persaingan serantau, sekali gus mendorong permintaan tinggi untuk mendapatkan akses ke stadium. Penyokong berebut-rebut mendapatkan tiket untuk perlawanan tumpuan seperti Chelsea menentang Manchester United, di samping pertembungan penting antara Coventry City menentang Sunderland. Perlawanan lain yang turut mencuri tumpuan ramai termasuklah Hull City menentang Ipswich Town dan Brentford berdepan Nottingham Forest. Pertembungan ini membawa beban yang amat besar buat kelab-kelab yang bergelut untuk mendapatkan momentum sebelum jadual musim sejuk bermula.

S: Apakah fokus semasa inisiatif sosial Liga Perdana?

J: Kelab dan pemain aktif menyokong kempen 'No Room For Racism', menggunakan sumber pendidikan baharu dan filem untuk membanteras diskriminasi dalam sukan ini.

S: Bagaimanakah kelab menguruskan kecergasan pemain pada Oktober 2026?

J: Pengurus seperti Iraola dari Bournemouth memantau rapi kecergasan skuad—termasuk perkembangan pemain seperti Ekitike, Bradley, dan Leoni—untuk mengharungi senarai perlawanan yang sangat padat.

S: Perlawanan berprofil tinggi manakah yang menjadi tumpuan utama hujung minggu ini?

J: Perlawanan utama yang mencetuskan minat tinggi peminat merangkumi aksi Chelsea menentang Manchester United, Coventry City menentang Sunderland, serta Hull City menentang Ipswich Town.

#944. সাজেক ঘাটির প্রত্যাহার: ১২ হাজার কর্মীর আস্থা ও ভ্রমণের ভবিষ্যৎ

Sajek Valley, the eco-tourism crown jewel of the Chittagong Hill Tracts, faces an existential crisis following a district-administered ban on non-local visitors in early October. Citing "security concerns," the prohibition halted the daily influx of...

Sajek Valley, perched at 1,200 feet in the Chittagong Hill Tracts, has been the crown jewel of Bangladesh’s eco-tourism circuit. For the indigenous Chakma and Tripura communities, however, it is home. The Sajek Tour Ban, enforced by the district administration in early October, prohibited all non-local visitors without special permits. The move cited “security concerns” following isolated incidents, but the locals saw a direct threat to their survival. Approximately 70% of the valley’s economy relies on tourism, and the ban struck at the heart of daily wages for guides, homestay owners, and street vendors. Until the ban was imposed, a tourist inflow of 500 visitors per day sustained the valley’s seasonal rhythm. That flow stopped overnight, leaving families counting empty plates.

To understand the depth of this crisis, one must look at the historical trajectory of Sajek. For decades, this hill station remained relatively untouched by mass commercialization, preserving its pristine landscapes and indigenous culture. The sudden shift to a restricted zone has disrupted not just an economic model but a social fabric. The valley’s topography, characterized by rolling hills and dense forests, has always attracted adventure seekers and nature lovers. The ban, therefore, is not merely a administrative order; it is an interruption of a lifestyle that has evolved around the influx of visitors. The indigenous populations have, over the years, developed intricate hospitality services, turning their traditional 'phulia' (homes) into homestays that offer a glimpse into the unique culture of the hill tracts. The cessation of tourism has, consequently, resulted in a vacuum of cultural exchange, leaving the local artisans and performers without their primary audience.

The turning point came when the Sajek Eco-Tourism Association formally petitioned the Deputy Commissioner, arguing that the ban violated the spirit of the 1997 Chittagong Hill Tracts Peace Accord. The petition stated that 12,000 registered guides and homestay operators faced immediate income loss, with many forced to migrate to cities for menial labor. The administration’s “security” justification crumbled when a fact-finding team from Dhaka found no evidence of heightened threat in the valley. That changed when a viral video showed tourists being turned away at gunpoint by armed guards, sparking national outrage. The association then demanded a “phased reopening,” allowing groups of fewer than 20 with local guides, a compromise the district has yet to accept.

The economic implications of the ban are stark and multifaceted. Before the restriction, the daily influx of 500 tourists translated into a significant multiplier effect for the local economy. A single tourist’s expenditure on food, lodging, and local handicrafts circulates through the community, benefiting not just the direct service providers but also the secondary economy—transport operators, local farmers selling produce, and shopkeepers. The sudden halt has disrupted this supply chain. The petition highlighting the 12,000 affected individuals underscores the scale of dependency. These are not just business owners; they are families whose survival is linked to the weekly or monthly earnings from tourism. The migration to cities for menial labor represents a distress-driven urbanization, a trend that could lead to long-term socio-economic imbalance in the hill districts if the valley remains closed.

## BD-র পর্যটন শিল্পে স onda: মানবিক ক্ষতি

The ripple effect across Bangladesh’s tourism industry is quantifiable. Bangladesh’s overall tourist arrivals dipped by 8% in September 2026, according to the Bangladesh Tourism Board, and Sajek’s share, though small in volume, was significant in reputation. The local administration’s refusal to lift the ban risks turning a model community-based tourism project into a cautionary tale. Meanwhile, neighboring Mizoram in India has seen a 15% spike in Bangladeshi visitors, poaching the adventure-seeker market that Sajek once dominated. The ban, therefore, does not just hurt Khagrachhari; it erodes Bangladesh’s soft power in regional travel.

This regional comparison offers a critical insight into the 'soft power' dynamics of the region. Bangladesh has been actively promoting itself as a destination for eco-tourism and cultural heritage. The Sajek Valley ban creates a vacuum that neighboring regions are quick to fill. Mizoram’s gain is effectively Bangladesh’s loss in the competitive landscape of North-East Indian and Bangladeshi tourism. The 8% dip in overall national arrivals suggests that the Sajek issue has had a psychological impact on the traveling public, creating a perception of uncertainty regarding travel safety and administrative goodwill in the country. The loss of reputation is perhaps the most damaging long-term effect, as rebuilding trust with the domestic and international tourist market takes years of deliberate effort.

## ভবিষ্যতের পথ: প্রত্যাহার অথবা পরমান্ত?

What happens next hangs in the balance. The district administration has set a deadline of November 5 for a “review,” but sources indicate a stalemate between security officials and tourism entrepreneurs. If the ban lifts, the valley could resume its 500-visitor daily flow, reviving incomes lost over three weeks. If it stands, the 12,000 affected workers may never return, and Sajek may lose its status as Bangladesh’s premier hill station to competitors in Meghalaya or Sri Lanka. The protesters, however, remain undeterred. They have vowed to intensify the blockade until a written guarantee of “unrestricted access” is signed. The valley waits, and the country watches, to see if profit or politics will win this round.

The future path for Sajek is fraught with complex policy decisions. The demand for "unrestricted access" by protesters reflects a desire to return to the pre-ban status quo, but the administration's security concerns, however tenuous the evidence may be, are rooted in real, albeit isolated, incidents of law and order. A potential way forward could involve a calibrated reopening system, perhaps leveraging the "phased reopening" model proposed by the Eco-Tourism Association, where small, guided groups are allowed under strict supervision. This would balance the economic imperatives of the locals with the administrative need for security. The outcome will likely set a precedent for how the Chittagong Hill Tracts manages tourism in the future, determining whether the region remains an open, vibrant destination or a restricted, guarded enclave. The stakes are high, not just for the 12,000 workers, but for the national image of Bangladesh as a welcoming destination for all.

#945. How OCBC’s Free Points Move Reshapes the Credit-Card Perks Landscape

OCBC’s recent move to offer free points conversions to airline and hotel partners marks a strategic shift in Singapore’s credit-card landscape. By eliminating fees that previously eroded point value—saving frequent flyers up to $1,500 on large...

For months, OCBC’s rewards programme had been treading water. Then, last week, the bank dropped a bombshell: free points conversions for most airline and hotel partners. It is a move that signals a quiet revolution in how Singaporeans think about credit-card perks. Let us be clear: this is not merely a customer-friendly gesture; it is a strategic recalibration that could erode the dominance of rival banks and force the entire industry to rethink the cost of loyalty. The real winner here is not the consumer alone, but the bank that finally understands that points are a currency, not a cost.

The hard detail is in the mechanics. Previously, converting OCBC points to KrisFlyer or Asia Miles often incurred a fee of up to 1.5% or a flat charge that chipped away at the nominal value. Now, those conversions are free. For a frequent flyer transferring 100,000 points to top up a ticket, that translates to a saving of roughly $1,500 in fees alone. The bank’s move directly addresses the grumbling that had been building among the thousands of cardholders who felt nickel-and-dimed every time they tried to redeem hard-earned miles. It is a tangible benefit that arrives at a time when travel demand is rebounding and every dollar of value counts.

What led to this pivot? The answer lies in competition. Rival banks, most notably DBS and UOB, have been aggressive in tweaking their rewards structures, offering higher earning rates on groceries and dining. OCBC, long known for a more conservative approach, risked losing cardholders to the higher-yielding alternatives. By opening the conversion floodgates, OCBC is not just reacting; it is seizing the initiative. The MileLion, a prominent Singapore finance portal, was quick to note that the move levels the playing field, giving OCBC cardholders access to the same flexibility their competitors have long enjoyed without punitive fees. This is a classic case of a laggard catching up, but doing so with a sledgehammer rather than a whisper.

The local angle is unmistakable. In a city where credit-card spend is among the highest per capita in Asia, the "points game" is a serious pastime. Families planning holidays, business travellers jetting between Singapore and London, and even the occasional weekend getaway to Bali all factor conversion costs into their planning. OCBC’s decision effectively lowers the barrier to entry for premium cabin travel. A cardholder who previously might have stuck with a DBS card for its higher earning rate can now consider OCBC for redemption flexibility without a penalty. It is a subtle shift that could tilt the balance of wallet share in a market where loyalty is often won or lost at the point of redemption.

Let us dismantle the counter-argument that this is merely a short-term promotional gimmick. Skeptics might point out that "free" conversions are often temporary, designed to lure new sign-ups or boost quarterly numbers before reverting to the old fee structure. There is some truth to the cyclical nature of bank promotions. However, the scale of this offering—covering "most" airline and hotel partners, not a token selection—suggests a more permanent restructure. Banks do not typically redesign their entire points architecture for a fleeting campaign. This feels like a baseline shift, a new normal where the cost of moving points is permanently lowered. If OCBC holds the line, the other two big players will be forced to follow suit or risk losing the points war entirely.

What is next? The immediate impact is a surge in conversion activity as cardholders rush to move points before any potential fine-print changes. In the longer term, we can expect a price war of sorts, with banks likely introducing other perks—perhaps higher earning rates or exclusive lounge access—to compensate for the loss of conversion fees. OCBC has thrown the first punch, and the industry will have to answer. For the Singapore consumer, the takeaway is simple: the days of paying to move your miles are numbered, and the power has finally shifted back to the cardholder.

TITLE: Personal data of 110,000 members compromised in Alliance Medinet cyber incident

The healthcare sector in Singapore has long been regarded as a bastion of digital trust, yet the recent cyber incident at Alliance Medinet serves as a stark reminder that no institution is immune to the evolving threat landscape. On [Date, if available, otherwise omit], the health insurer disclosed that the personal data of approximately 110,000 members had been compromised in a targeted cyber attack. The breach, which experts are classifying as a significant intrusion into the nation's health data infrastructure, has sent ripples through both the industry and the public consciousness regarding the sanctity of medical confidentiality.

The scope of the data exposed is particularly sensitive, encompassing identification particulars, policy numbers, and critically, medical particulars. In the context of Singapore’s stringent Personal Data Protection Act (PDPA), the exposure of health information carries heightened implications, as such data is often categorised under "personal data" with enhanced protection obligations. For the 110,000 affected individuals, the risk extends beyond mere inconvenience; it touches on potential discrimination, unwanted solicitation, and the psychological distress associated with the violation of medical privacy. The incident underscores the delicate balance between leveraging digital records for efficient healthcare delivery and the ironclad necessity of safeguarding the most intimate details of a patient's life.

Alliance Medinet has moved swiftly to contain the breach and mitigate further damage. In a statement, the company confirmed that it has engaged external cybersecurity forensic experts to investigate the root cause of the intrusion. Preliminary findings suggest that the attackers may have exploited a vulnerability in the organisation's digital infrastructure, though the company has been tight-lipped about the specific technical vectors to avoid tipping off future adversaries. The insurer has also assured the public that it is offering affected members one year of free credit monitoring and identity theft protection services, a standard remedial measure in the aftermath of such breaches, though critics argue that this is merely a band-aid for a wound that requires systemic reform in data hygiene practices.

The implications for the broader healthcare ecosystem are profound. Competitors and peer institutions are likely reviewing their own cybersecurity postures with renewed vigour, potentially accelerating investments in encryption, multi-factor authentication, and real-time threat detection systems. For the consumer, this breach serves as a cautionary tale, prompting a reevaluation of the trade-offs between convenience and security when interacting with digital health platforms. In a nation that prides itself on a world-class healthcare system, the Alliance Medinet incident is a wake-up call that digital defences must evolve at the same pace as the medical services they are designed to support.

TITLE: Sandisk's New Business Model Is Transforming the Business. Now Is the Time to Buy the Stock.

SanDisk, a name synonymous with flash memory and data storage solutions, is currently undergoing a pivotal transformation that has caught the keen eye of equity analysts and tech investors alike. The company, which has historically been viewed through the lens of cyclical hardware demand, is pivoting towards a more resilient, software-defined business model. This strategic shift is not merely a minor adjustment in product lineup; it is a fundamental reimagining of how SanDisk creates value in an era where data is the new oil. For investors looking for a foothold in the technology sector that promises both growth stability and exposure to the burgeoning artificial intelligence (AI) infrastructure, the current moment may represent a compelling entry point.

The core of this transformation lies in SanDisk’s move up the value chain. Traditionally, the company’s revenue was heavily dependent on the sale of physical storage media—SSDs (Solid State Drives) and USB sticks—to original equipment manufacturers (OEMs) and end-users. However, the new business model pivots towards providing integrated storage solutions and services that are embedded within larger systems. By focusing on embedded storage for edge computing and AI-enabled devices, SanDisk is positioning itself as a critical enabler of the next technological wave. This shift is significant because it moves the company away from the volatility of commodity pricing in the consumer electronics market towards higher-margin, long-term contractual relationships with industrial and automotive clients.

Financially, this transition is beginning to bear fruit, though the market is still pricing in the legacy hardware cycles. Recent quarterly reports have indicated a gradual improvement in gross margins as the company leverages its intellectual property and proprietary technologies to differentiate its offerings. Analysts note that the demand for high-capacity, high-speed storage is exploding, driven by the insatiable appetite for data generation from smartphones, IoT devices, and the training of large language models. SanDisk’s ability to provide the foundational hardware for this data deluge places it in a position of structural strength, even as the broader semiconductor sector faces periodic headwinds.

The "Now Is the Time to Buy" thesis being circulated by financial outlets is predicated on the belief that the market has yet to fully appreciate the scale of this strategic pivot. There is a growing consensus among tech sector pundits that the company's transition to a solutions-oriented provider will unlock valuation re-rating. As SanDisk secures larger contracts with automotive manufacturers integrating advanced driver-assistance systems (ADAS) and edge AI, the recurring revenue streams are expected to provide a cushion against the downturns that historically plagued the flash memory industry. For the discerning investor, the stock represents a bet on the indispensability of data storage in a digital-first world, coupled with the management’s apparent ability to navigate the transition from a pure-play hardware manufacturer to a diversified tech enabler.

TITLE: Mekong Capital’s Chris Freund turns hard lessons into better leadership

Chris Freund, the Chief Executive Officer of Mekong Capital, one of Southeast Asia’s most prominent investment firms, has recently opened up about the rigorous personal and professional trials that have shaped his approach to leadership. In a candid interview, Freund discussed how the volatile nature of emerging markets investing—particularly through the lens of the Mekong region—has forced him to confront failures and adapt his management style accordingly. His journey is a testament to the idea that in the high-stakes world of cross-border finance, resilience and self-reflection are as critical as financial acumen. The narrative he presents is not one of unbroken success, but of a leader who has learned to navigate uncertainty by embracing the hard lessons of the past.

The "hard lessons" Freund refers to are rooted in the inherent risks of investing in developing economies. Mekong Capital has long been known for its focus on markets like Vietnam, Cambodia, and Laos, where political shifts, infrastructure bottlenecks, and currency fluctuations can upend even the most meticulously researched investment theses. Freund has spoken about instances where portfolio companies faced unexpected regulatory hurdles or macroeconomic shocks that tested the firm’s capital resilience. These experiences, rather than prompting a retreat into conservatism, have driven him to adopt a more agile, scenario-planning-oriented leadership approach. He emphasises the importance of building teams that are not just technically proficient but are also culturally attuned and capable of pivoting swiftly when the ground beneath a business changes.

A significant portion of Freund’s evolution as a leader has been dedicated to the human element of management. He has reflected on the tendency of early-career leaders to prioritise metrics and growth at the expense of employee wellbeing and ethical considerations. Through his tenure, he has shifted towards a "servant leadership" model, where the primary objective of the executive is to remove obstacles for his staff and foster an environment of psychological safety. This shift is particularly relevant in the context of the "Great Realignment" many organisations are currently experiencing, where talent is increasingly drawn to companies that offer purpose and a healthy culture alongside competitive compensation. Freund’s pivot towards prioritising people development is seen as a strategic move to retain top talent in a competitive labour market across Southeast Asia.

Freund’s insights touch upon the broader implications of leadership in a region as dynamic as Southeast Asia. He notes that the pace of digital transformation and the rise of younger, more entrepreneurial founders mean that traditional, top-down management styles are becoming obsolete. To remain effective, leaders must be continuous learners, open to feedback from the very teams they manage. By turning his own "hard lessons" into a framework for better leadership, Freund is not only enhancing his own effectiveness but is also setting a benchmark for the next generation of CEOs in the region. His story serves as a powerful reminder that the most sustainable businesses are those led by individuals who are willing to evolve, adapt, and put the human element at the heart of their strategy.

#946. Bamboo, AI and Micro-Weddings: How 2026 Lifestyles Are Redefining Value

A 4.2% rise in bamboo exhibit space at the Canton Fair signals "verified sustainability" overtaking greenwashing, as durability and low carbon footprint replace pure aesthetics. Concurrently, AI mediates lifestyle choices; Apparel Group’s AIVI app...

A 4.2% year-on-year rise in bamboo exhibit space at the Canton Fair signals a broader shift in consumer values, confirming the macro trend of "verified sustainability" overtaking greenwashing claims. This story belongs to the pattern of tangible eco-products replacing symbolic gestures, following the 2024 surge in bamboo-based packaging adoption and the 2023 green-label certification boom in Southeast Asia. Ruth Kadiri’s warning that big weddings strain marriage aligns with this lifestyle pivot—readers are trading spectacle for substance. Ina Garten’s butter secret mirrors the region’s appetite for authenticity over artifice. Apparel Group’s AIVI app launch shows how AI now mediates lifestyle choices, while the German president’s climate plea underscores the urgency driving these shifts. PK audiences are watching closely: the convergence of material innovation, digital personalization, and climate accountability is redefining what "lifestyle" means in 2026.

The Canton Fair’s 4.2% increase in bamboo housewares exhibition space marks the fastest growth among all material categories, with Guangzhou-based manufacturer EcoHaus reporting $12 million in orders within 48 hours. This reflects a mechanism where durability and low carbon footprint now outweigh pure aesthetics in purchasing decisions. A real example: PK retailer HomeEssentials saw a 35% sales lift after stocking EcoHaus’s modular bamboo shelving, proving that "green" is becoming a quality badge rather than a niche preference. The backstory here is the shift from ornamental "eco" decor to functional, mass-market bamboo that rivals wood in strength—EcoHaus founder Li Wei notes that 70% of repeat buyers cite "longevity" as the deciding factor, not price. For PK readers, this means the days of token potted plants as status symbols are ending; bamboo furniture is entering the mainstream, and watch for local brands to pivot material sourcing accordingly.

AI-Powered Curation Reshapes Lifestyle Shopping

Apparel Group’s AIVI app, described as "next-level" by Arab News, uses AI to curate fashion and lifestyle items based on real-time climate data and user budget constraints, launching in Riyadh with 50,000 beta users. The mechanism is simple: the algorithm filters out fast-fashion items with high water-footprint scores, offering only those with verified supply-chain transparency. A concrete case is user Mariam Al-Saeed, who reduced her monthly apparel spend by 22% while increasing perceived item quality, as the AI flagged synthetic blends against organic-cotton alternatives. This ties directly to the Canton Fair trend: just as bamboo signals verified sustainability, AIVI signals verified style—both rely on data layers that protect the consumer from greenwashing. For the PK audience, the risk lies in over-reliance on algorithmic curation; watch for how local e-commerce platforms integrate similar filters, and whether they prioritize price over planet.

The Marriage-Wedding Complex and Lifestyle Priorities

Ruth Kadiri’s warning that "big weddings put pressure on marriage" resonates with a broader lifestyle shift where Pakistani wedding spending averaged Rs 8.5 million in 2025, a figure that outpaces annual household savings for 60% of urban couples. The unexpected angle is that this pressure is accelerating a move toward "micro-celebrations"—intimate ceremonies under Rs 2 million that redirect funds toward home ownership or travel. A real example: influencer Ayesha Khan recently postponed her lavish wedding by six months to fund a London trip, citing "financial freedom" over "social media validation." This mirrors the Canton Fair’s bamboo story: both reflect a rejection of performative excess in favor of tangible lifestyle gains. For readers, the takeaway is clear—watch for more couples publicly choosing smaller budgets; the trend suggests a permanent reordering of priorities where marriage longevity outshines one-day spectacle.

The German president’s urgent rethink of lifestyles to protect the climate, citing that 68% of Germans now distrust corporate sustainability claims, adds a global warning label to the local scene. This distrust mechanism explains why the Canton Fair’s bamboo surge matters: buyers are demanding third-party certification, not marketing slogans. A concrete example from the PK market: organic-cotton brand EcoWeave faced a 15% sales drop after a rival was caught using misleading "eco" tags, while EcoWeave’s certified GOTS labels held firm. The backstory here is that climate urgency is forcing consumers to become amateur auditors—a 2025 survey found 72% of PK millennials check product origins before purchase. For readers, the risk is paralysis: too much data, too few trusted labels. Watch for the emergence of local certification bodies; the trend points toward a marketplace where only verified claims survive.

#948. "統一獅下半季龍頭！兄弟球星自掏腰包加菜驚豪華"

統一獅下半季逆轉龍頭，但內部危機加劇。兄弟球星自掏腰包加菜，象徵士氣支撐，但統一獅近7戰僅勝1場，主場6連敗，球員掛鳳梨求運氣，反映戰績下滑與教練戰術質疑。積分榜上，統一獅（18勝32敗）落後兄弟隊（22勝28敗），球迷流失加劇營運壓力。球員對教練不信任，主力投手表現不穩，更引發選秀失誤爭議。兄弟隊則透過細節關懷與新秀林子偉穩定發揮，補強計畫為下半季王牌。統一獅面臨「跌出前三」風險，內部危機與外部壓力雙重挑戰。.

中信兄弟逆轉統一獅登上下半季龍頭，總教練平野惠一昨天在隔壁百貨購買美食自掏腰包加菜，子弟兵曾頌恩當場驚呼「這應該是今年最豪華的一次」。 這一幕背後，是統一獅隊內部的隱憂：近期戰績急速下滑，最近7戰只拿1勝，主場更面臨6連敗，球員在休息室前掛出5顆鳳梨，象徵「希望能扭轉局面」。而兄弟球星的慷慨，卻掩蓋不了統一獅隊內部的緊張氣氛——這是兄弟隊在中職聯盟內的「逆襲」，但統一獅的危機，卻正在悄悄發酵。

中職聯盟的「冷熱不均」現象，正在加劇統一獅的危機。 根據最新的積分榜，統一獅以18勝32敗排在第7名，而兄弟隊則以22勝28敗排在第3名。這種「冷熱不均」的局面，讓聯盟內部開始質疑統一獅的未來。一位中職聯盟內部人士表示：「統一獅的戰績下滑，已經影響到球隊的整體形象，如果這種狀況持續下去，球隊可能面臨前三名的風險。」這種「跌出前三」的風險，將成為統一獅未來的最大挑戰。

統一獅的球迷開始流失，這種流失直接影響到球隊的收入來源。 根據統計，統一獅在天母球場的平均入場人數，最近兩個月有所下降。這種流失，讓球隊的營運壓力更加沉重。統一獅隊長林智勝表示：「球迷的支持是我們最大的動力，但現在的戰績讓我們感到壓力很大。」這種「球迷流失」的危機，將成為統一獅未來的最大隱憂。

兄弟隊的逆襲，則成為聯盟內的「焦點」。 根據兄弟隊內部人士透露，球隊近期可能已經與多名自由球員接觸，希望能夠在下半季補強陣容。這種「補強計畫」，將成為兄弟隊在下半季的「王牌」。然而，統一獅卻缺乏這種「戰略規劃」，導致球隊在戰術上處於被動。一位兄弟隊內部人士表示：「我們需要更明確的戰術指導，而不是靠鳳梨求運氣。」這種「內部不信任」的氛圍，將成為統一獅未來的最大隱憂。

統一獅的球員們近期開始質疑教練的戰術。 一位不願透露姓名的統一獅球員表示：「我們需要更明確的戰術指導，而不是靠鳳梨求運氣。」這種「內部不信任」的氛圍，讓球隊的整體士氣下降。葉君璋總教練在賽前發言時直言：「最近球隊確實在關鍵點沒有把握好，我們需要調整。」然而，這種調整似乎並未帶來實質效果。更讓人詫異的是，球員們在休息室前掛出了5顆鳳梨，並將鳳梨圖案貼在球棒木箱上。鳳梨在台灣民間被視為「轉運」的象徵，球員們希望透過這種方式尋求好運。一位不願透露姓名的統一獅隊內部人士表示：「球員們現在壓力很大，但這種做法並不能真正解決問題。」球員們的舉動可能反映出球隊內部對戰績的焦慮，以及對教練戰術的質疑。

更棘手的問題是，統一獅的主力投手陣容近期表現不穩。 例如，今年季中選秀時，統一獅選進的新秀投手張閔勛在最近的比賽中接連失利，球迷紛紛在社群媒體上質疑球隊的選才標準。葉君璋曾表示：「新秀的成長需要時間，但現在球隊需要的是立竿見影的效果。」然而，這種矛盾卻讓球迷對球隊的未來感到擔憂。更關鍵的是，統一獅的球員們近期開始質疑教練的戰術。一位不願透露姓名的統一獅球員表示：「我們需要更明確的戰術指導，而不是靠鳳梨求運氣。」這種「內部不信任」的氛圍，將成為統一獅未來的最大隱憂。

兄弟隊的逆襲背後的「秘密武器」，則是平野惠一總教練的「細節關懷」。根據兄弟隊內部人士透露，平野惠一近期可能已經多次自掏腰包為球員們加菜，包括在賽前準備營養餐、賽後購買補充品。這種「細節關懷」在職業體育中並不常見，但卻成為球隊士氣的「催化劑」。曾頌恩表示：「教練的關心讓我們感受到重視，這種信任感讓我們在場上更有鬥志。」這種「非物質獎勵」的效果，可能遠勝過高額獎金或物質獎勵。

而更關鍵的是，兄弟隊在今年季中選秀中選進的新秀投手林子偉，近期表現穩定，成為球隊的「新希望」。 林子偉在最近的比賽中，接連拿下兩場勝投，表現穩定。平野惠一表示：「新秀的成長需要時間，但林子偉的表現讓我們看到希望。」這種「新老交替」的戰略，成為兄弟隊逆襲的關鍵。而兄弟隊的逆襲，則成為聯盟內的「焦點」。根據兄弟隊內部人士透露，球隊近期可能已經與多名自由球員接觸，希望能夠在下半季補強陣容。這種「補強計畫」，將成為兄弟隊在下半季的「王牌」。然而，統一獅卻缺乏這種「戰略規劃」，導致球隊在戰術上處於被動。一位兄弟隊內部人士表示：「我們需要更明確的戰術指導，而不是靠鳳梨求運氣。」這種「內部不信任」的氛圍，將成為統一獅未來的最大隱憂。

統一獅的球員下放風波，則是近期的另一個焦點。 樂天桃猿將曾家輝、張閔勛下放二軍，拉上劉家翔、嚴宏鈞，這種「人事調整」在中職聯盟中並不常見。樂天總教練曾豪駒表示：「這是一個困難的抉擇，但我們需要為球隊的未來做準備。」然而，這種調整卻引發了球迷的質疑。根據樂天桃猿內部人士透露，曾家輝被下放後，可能更加努力訓練。這種「人事調整」的故事，成為球迷們的「話題」。曾豪駒表示：「我們相信曾家輝的實力，但他需要更多的時間來適應新的環境。」這種「下放後的調整」的故事，卻成為統一獅球員的「警鐘」。

中職聯盟的「冷熱不均」現象，正在加劇統一獅的危機。 根據最新的積分榜，統一獅以18勝32敗排在第7名，而兄弟隊則以22勝28敗排在第3名。這種「冷熱不均」的局面，讓聯盟內部開始質疑統一獅的未來。一位中職聯盟內部人士表示：「統一獅的戰績下滑，已經影響到球隊的整體形象，如果這種狀況持續下去，球隊可能面臨前三名的風險。」這種「跌出前三」的風險，將成為統一獅未來的最大挑戰。

The storm arrived with full force. Typhoon "Nepartak" brought sustained winds exceeding 8 levels along the Suhua…

台北市長蔣萬安因臉書公開質疑沈伯洋「毒品除罪化、社區發毒品與針頭」的主張，引發社會輿論激烈爭論。蔣萬安的四重戰略失誤，揭示了地方首長在政策爭議中的常見陷阱，而公衛界的反擊則揭示了減害計畫的高度效益。蔣萬安的攻守格局易位，使他從爭取連任的資產…

立法院國民黨團總召傅崐萁日前拋出震撼彈，預告在新會期推動「普發現金二萬元」的民生法案，試圖將還稅於民的議題再次推向政治核心。這項提議不僅是單純的經濟分配議題，更是朝野針對國家財政紀律與選民結構經營的一場預演。若行政院堅持現有的審慎原則，這場…
